# Skills · T · part 59

918 entries, test-after-change to test-automation. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **test-after-change**: Always test new or changed functionality in landly-client right after writing it — before saying the work is done. Use after ANY code change in src/ (new hook, service method, type/enum, helper, store, component, screen, bug fix, refactor) — "implement X", "add Y", "fix Z", "refactor …", "wire up the endpoint". Says what to test per layer with the project's test helpers, how to prove the test catches a regression, which commands must pass (tests, changed-file coverage, lint, build) and how to report it. ([Mazot/landly-client](https://github.com/Mazot/landly-client/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/test-after-change))
- **test-after-dev**: Performs comprehensive unit and E2E testing based on spec after development. Invoke when development is complete, user asks to test, or before committing changes. ([JustinMoFeng/AI-learning-system](https://github.com/JustinMoFeng/AI-learning-system/tree/HEAD/.trae/skills/test-after-dev))
- **test-against-real-shapes**: Choose the SUBJECT of a test or measurement so it is as close to real data as the question allows, even when the data is synthetic. Use when writing a test, fixture or probe for anything the engine also does — physics, decode, rendering, animation — and especially when a fixture is about to be invented rather than taken from a real file. Also use when a fixture's result is steering a design decision. ([PinKushin/Tf2DemoSalvage](https://github.com/PinKushin/Tf2DemoSalvage/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/test-against-real-shapes) · ★1)

- **test-agent**: Adversarial testing agent. Takes natural language test targets ("test sync conflict resolution, semantic search, markdown tables"), generates adversarial scenarios, executes them, and reports structured verdicts comparing actual vs expected behavior. Use when the user says "test X", "can you test", "X needs testing", "break this", "QA this", or describes features/fixes that need adversarial validation. Unlike /verify (which runs existing test suites), /test-agent generates NEW ephemeral test scenarios designed to break things.
